```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

```
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {

```
```
public class IsUndirectedGraph {
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;

        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

```

```
public class IsUndirectedGraph {
    //@  requires adjacencyMat != null;
    //@  requires (\forall int r; 0 <= r && r < adjacencyMat.length; adjacencyMat[r] != null && adjacencyMat[r].length == adjacencyMat.length);
    //@  assigns \nothing;
```
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i,j; 0 < i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat.length; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);  
    //@  pure
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= nVert;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int a,b; 0 - 1 <= a && a <= b || b < i; adjacencyMat[a][b] == adjacencyMat[b][a]);  
        //@ loop_writes \nothing;
        //@ decreases nVert - i;

            //@ maintaining i+1 <= j && j <= nVert;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int a,b; 0 < a && a < b && b < i; adjacencyMat[a][b] == adjacencyMat[b][a]);  
            //@ maintaining (\forall int j0; i+1 <= j0 && j0 < j; adjacencyMat[i][j0] == adjacencyMat[j0][i]);

                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    //@  requires (\forall int r; 0 <= r && r < adjacencyMat.length; adjacencyMat[r] != null && adjacencyMat[r].length == adjacencyMat.length);
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i,j; 0 < i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 - 1 <= j && j < adjacencyMat.length; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);  
    //@  pure
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= nVert;
 
        //@ loop_writes \nothing;
        //@ decreases nVert - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i+1 <= j && j <= nVert;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int a,b; 0 <= a && a < b || b < i; adjacencyMat[a][b] == adjacencyMat[b][a]);  
            //@ maintaining (\forall int j0; i+1 <= j0 && j0 < j; adjacencyMat[i][j0] == adjacencyMat[j0][i]);
            //@ loop_writes \nothing;
            //@ decreases nVert - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

    //@  requires (\forall int r; 0 <= r && r < adjacencyMat.length; adjacencyMat[r] != null && adjacencyMat[r].length == adjacencyMat.length);
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\exists int i,j; 0 - 1 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 - 1 <= j && j <= adjacencyMat.length; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);  
    //@  pure
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= nVert;
 
        //@ loop_writes \nothing;
        //@ decreases nVert - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i+1 <= j && j <= nVert;
 
            //@ maintaining (\forall int j0; i+1 <= j0 && j0 < j; adjacencyMat[i][j0] == adjacencyMat[j0][i]);
            //@ loop_writes \nothing;
            //@ decreases nVert - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}
